Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the planetary object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 925. The moduli space has dimension at most 570 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the planetary space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 925 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 570. QED.
